## Acceptance and execution sequence

Assess separately: supported physical model/source agreement; complete coupled
cold/freeze/thaw cycle with actual receiver; independent water/energy and
state/restart/rejection correctness; absolute cost and scaling. Missing required
evidence blocks acceptance. Functional success with excessive cost is
`FUNCTIONAL_EVIDENCE_PASS / PERFORMANCE_HOLD`, never production qualification.

Internal sequence: verify custody and budget; source-backed model and compact
cost allocation; minimum successor clauses with old definition bytes preserved;
two independent authority reviews; contract-derived expected-red tests and
pre-implementation gates; reusable Rust and actual local receiver; focused
corrections; frozen final matrix; conservation/restart/cost evidence; same-reviewer
verification; preservation and disposition. These stages do not require renewed
owner approval within the adopted boundary.

M1 retains positive-area foliage and basal/hydraulic exchange in a justified
cold physiological domain, one bulk water/enthalpy reservoir per occupancy,
liquid and ice vapor exchange, freezing/melting and finite liquid release.
Two distinct occupancies retain vertical ordering and independent ground-snow
ownership. Original two 0.018 kg/m² liquid inputs remain historical evidence;

### Preimplementation cost allocation

Select the reviewed 21-coordinate energy/reservoir block plus two unchanged
four-coordinate hydraulic blocks. Persistent new physical state is exactly two
f64 values (M,H), 16 bytes per occupancy; diagnostic identity and existing owner
state are separate. D and diagnosed phases are support-local. A naive enlarged
joint solve has 33 coordinates. Finite-difference assembly in the selected
form uses 21 energy probe evaluations plus the base evaluation, followed by
4 probes per hydraulic block and their base evaluations. Dense elimination
work scales approximately as 21³+2*4³ versus 33³ (9389 versus 35937 cubic work
units), with the expensive constitutive work still present in each energy
probe. This is an allocation comparison, not a measured speedup or proof that
absolute cost fits. A 21×21 f64 Jacobian is3528 bytes before vectors/scratch;
scratch is reused per lane/support rather than retained per trial.

Retain the existing canonical Newton limits of50 iterations and20 backtracking
halvings (`openwepp-land-surface-energy/src/numerics.rs`); no new outer retry.
A worst-case iteration also incurs the bounded line-search evaluations. Charge
complete physical maps according to existing caller definitions, separately
from Newton/probe counts. Every caller/receiver evaluation and failed attempt
remains measured. The eight-map ceiling is unchanged.

At60-second supports a72-hour fixture has4320 accepted supports per lane;
longer canonical supports may reduce that count, but no coarsening is selected
for performance. Phase crossings occur within the implicit constitutive solve;
external forcing/topology events retain canonical splitting and the60-second
floor. Store only current/transaction candidate owner state and reusable scratch,
O(occupancies×OFEs), with no numerical history proportional to simulated time.
Compact correctness traces are evidence outside the timed kernel. Receiver
routing, typed receipt validation and actual owner use are included in the
coupled timing; hashing and JSON packaging remain outside its hot path and are
reported in end-to-end cost. There are no measured timing/memory results yet.

Static planning only — adviser `/root/cold_domain_adviser` derived a conditional
steady-ice contradiction, not global infeasibility or an acceptance verdict.
Assumptions: both occupancies retain positive dry shade (fw<1); each ice
reservoir has constant T within258.15..273.15K while mass may evolve; both wet
vapor fluxes are nonnegative; no liquid drainage; fixed cold snow H/v,SW and
rain. Let T=Tcan,rho=p/(287.05T),tau=exp(-.8*1.35)=.3395955256, and B_i be
area-weighted sigma*Tcomponent^4. The inspected equations give:

```
Hcan = rho*cp*(T-Ta)/ra - Hsnow
Ecan = rho*(qcan-qa)/rv - Esnow
LWcan = (1-tau^2)*(LWdown + sigma*Tsnow^4 - Bupper - Blower)
Ecan >= 4.8716704257e-5 - .01101655035/T       [qcan >= 0]
```

Actual fixed cold rain capture is
8e-6*[a+(1-a)*.87*a]=6.711811792e-6kg/m2/s, a=.73*tanh(1.35).
Isothermal-ice Q=Ls*E+hi*I retains at most2.451758W/m2 incoming-freezing heat.
The adviser bounds LWcan<=43.458019W/m2 and
Hcan+2501000*Ecan<=43.458019+2.451758, implying Tcan<=262.679587K.
Positive dry-shade energy with Tleaf>=263.15 instead gives
LWcan<=23.991410-43943.815125*(263.15/Tcan-1), implying Tcan>=263.419905K
under the same assumptions. Full wet cover, evolving ice sensible storage, or
deposition invalidate the conditional proof. No simulation ran and no source
or forcing changed. Actual original60 and both frozen continuous cases remain
required; this adviser calculation cannot replace them or justify retuning.
